<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离散时间线性系统中，当面对一个紧致集合（而非单一系统）时，鲁棒镇定所需的反馈律最小复杂性问题，即确定是否存在以及需要何种类型的反馈控制器来镇定整个系统集合。

Method: 通过构造性证明方法证明存在动态状态反馈控制器实现全局渐近镇定；通过反例或构造性论证证明静态非线性或线性动态反馈的局限性；并将结果推广到鲁棒指数镇定（给定衰减率）情形；针对多面体系统集合提出反馈律设计的算法。

Result: 证明了对于任意紧致可镇定系统集合存在动态状态反馈控制器实现全局渐近镇定；证明了某些紧致集合不存在非线性静态或线性动态反馈可实现该目标（即鲁棒镇定一般需要既非线性又动态的反馈）；扩展了鲁棒指数镇定结果；为多面体集合提供了设计算法。

Conclusion: 鲁棒镇定问题中，反馈律的复杂性与系统集合的复杂性相关；一般情况下，实现鲁棒镇定需要同时具备非线性和动态特性的反馈控制器，这为实际控制器的设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of robust stabilization of linear input-state systems in discrete time. We prove that for any compact set of stabilizable systems, there exists a dynamic state-feedback controller that globally asymptotically stabilizes all systems in the set. In addition, we show that for some compact sets of stabilizable systems, no nonlinear static or linear dynamic state-feedback law can achieve this task. This proves that, in general, robust stabilization requires a feedback law that is both nonlinear and dynamic. We extend our study to robust exponential stabilization with a given rate of decay. Finally, for polytopic sets of systems, we introduce an algorithm for the design of robust feedback laws.

</details>


### [100] [Constrained Spatial Pricing of On-Street Parking with Bayesian Demand Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19015)
*Ananya Kale,Mohit Apte*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以占用率为目标的路径收费（curb pricing）方法，利用旧金山SFpark分区数据估计分层贝叶斯需求模型中的价格弹性（后验均值-0.241，95%CI: [-0.325, -0.176]），并将弹性后验分布嵌入含空间耦合、时间价格变化限制、相邻价格差约束及软性收入下限的非线性规划定价模型，使用Ipopt求解。通过期望损失与CVaR目标在后验场景上评估，基于后验的CVaR策略在测试数据上以概率1.00优于SFpark阈值规则与历史费率、以概率0.85优于文献校准的鲁棒策略。Epsilon约束前沿分析显示后验校准改变了有效定价集合，在Fillmore、Mission和Marina的60个区段窗口验证了占用率带表现、收入与空间差异之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于占用率的路径定价（如旧金山SFpark试点）多依赖本地阈值规则，未考虑空间替代效应以及需求响应的不确定性，导致定价方案可能次优。

Method: 构建分层贝叶斯需求模型从SFpark细分数据中估计弹性后验分布（部分池化），将后验样本嵌入空间耦合的约束非线性规划定价问题（含时间变化限制、相邻价格差距及软性收入地板），用Ipopt求解，并分别在期望损失和CVaR目标下进行联合后验评估；采用Epsilon约束方法生成前沿并进行多区段实证验证。

Result: 弹性后验均值为-0.241（CI: [-0.325, -0.176]）；后验CVaR策略在保留测试场景上以概率1.00优于SFpark阈值规则和历史费率，以概率0.85优于文献校准的鲁棒策略；后验校准改变了Epsilon约束前沿的有效定价集合，并在60个区段窗口中展现占用率表现、收入与空间差异的权衡。

Conclusion: 将需求参数的不确定性显式纳入空间耦合的定价优化中，能够产生比现有阈值规则和历史费率更优的路径收费策略，为占用率目标下的城市路缘定价提供了基于后验分布的可操作性决策框架。

Abstract: Occupancy-targeted curb pricing, including San Francisco's SFpark pilot, often relies on local threshold rules that do not account for spatial substitution or uncertainty in demand response. We estimate parking-price elasticities from disaggregated SFpark data using a hierarchical Bayesian demand model and use the resulting posterior distribution in a spatially coupled constrained pricing model. Partial pooling across districts gives a posterior mean elasticity of -0.241 with a 95% credible interval of [-0.325, -0.176]. The pricing problem is formulated as a constrained nonlinear program with spatial coupling, temporal price-change limits, neighbor price gaps, and a soft revenue floor, and is solved with Ipopt. Expected-loss and CVaR objectives are evaluated over joint posterior draws rather than a fixed elasticity. On held-out posterior scenarios, the posterior-based CVaR policy has a lower objective than the SFpark threshold rule and historical tariff with posterior probability 1.00, and than a literature-calibrated robust policy with probability 0.85. Epsilon-constraint frontiers show that posterior calibration changes the efficient pricing set. Results from 60 district-windows across Fillmore, Mission, and Marina also report the associated tradeoffs in occupancy-band performance, revenue, and spatial disparity.

</details>


### [101] [A Mini-Batch Counterexample to Last-Iterate Convergence in Definable Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19074)
*Weiwei Kong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个反例，否证了Bolte & Pauwels (2021)中关于mini-batch随机逼近（具有可定义势函数）收敛猜想的Remark 12。通过两个凸分段仿射（半代数）函数和特殊步长序列，构造出迭代点几乎必然遍历整个格点集但不收敛、累积集恰为[-1,1]的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bolte & Pauwels (2021)在Remark 12中提出了关于mini-batch随机逼近方法收敛性的猜想。作者旨在验证或否证该猜想，通过构造具体反例来阐明该猜想的边界条件。

Method: 构造两个定义在R上的凸分段仿射（半代数）函数作为随机逼近的两种取舍函数；选择确定性非增块步长序列满足α_k=o(1/log k)；在每个聚合批次场上选择容许的最小范数选择；使得连续块上的迭代形成嵌套二进格点上的惰性反射随机游走。利用端点覆盖时间估计、Markov不等式和第一Borel-Cantelli引理证明几乎所有充分晚的块其迭代都会访问整个格点。

Result: 证明几乎必然地，足够晚的每个块的迭代都会访问其完整格点集；迭代点保持在[-1,1]内但不收敛；累积集恰好为[-1,1]，在此区间上平均目标函数为常数；且构造满足∑α_k²=∞。

Conclusion: 该构造构成对Bolte & Pauwels (2021) Remark 12收敛猜想的反例，表明即使满足可定义势函数、平方可积步长发散等条件，mini-batch随机逼近迭代仍可能不收敛。

Abstract: We give a counterexample to the convergence conjecture in Remark 12 of [Bolte & Pauwels, 2021] for mini-batch stochastic approximation with definable potentials. The construction uses two convex piecewise-affine, hence semialgebraic, summands on $\mathbb{R}$. We choose a deterministic nonincreasing block stepsize sequence satisfying $α_k = o(1/\log k)$ and an admissible minimum-norm selection from each aggregate batch field. On successive blocks, the iterates form lazy reflected random walks on nested dyadic lattices. An explicit endpoint-cover-time estimate, Markov's inequality, and the first Borel-Cantelli lemma imply that almost surely every sufficiently late block's iterates visit their entire lattice. Consequently, the iterates remain in $[-1,1]$ but do not converge, and their accumulation set is exactly $[-1,1]$, on which the averaged objective is constant. Finally, the construction has $\sum_k α_k^2 =\infty$. Both Chat-GPT 5.6 (Sol) and Gemini Pro 3.1 (DeepThink) were used in the development and drafting of this result.

</details>


### [102] [Constrained minmax density transportation for linear parabolic PDEs: a numerical optimal control perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19170)
*Siddhartha Ganguly,Vaibhav Upadhyay,Kenji Kashima,Debasish Chatterjee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于带噪声的线性抛物型偏微分方程（特别是热方程）的minmax约束密度控制的数值最优控制框架，通过有限差分离散化和凸半无限规划（SIP）求解，并建立了精确解的理论保证和高效优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带噪声的PDE密度控制问题在实际中广泛存在（如热扩散、物质输运等），但如何在有限时间内将初始密度传输到目标密度，同时抵御无限多种干扰实现、并满足路径约束，是一个具有挑战性的问题。现有方法难以同时保证对无限扰动族的约束满足性并提供计算上可处理的求解方案。

Method: 1) 利用有限差分近似离散PDE中的空间导数，将PDE转化为时间上的常微分方程组；2) 对控制和扰动轨迹空间进行有限参数化；3) 将最优控制问题在温和假设下转化为凸半无限规划（SIP）；4) 利用凸SIP理论的新数值工具，建立精确解的存在性保证（能处理无限扰动族下的约束满足问题）；5) 提出基于优化的高效算法来恢复这些精确解。

Result: 建立了针对无限扰动族约束满足的精确解理论保证，开发了基于优化的计算高效求解算法，并通过综合数值算例验证了所提方法的有效性与正确性。

Conclusion: 该工作为带噪声线性抛物型PDE的minmax密度控制提供了一套既有理论保证又计算高效的完整求解框架，凸SIP工具的应用使得对无限干扰的鲁棒性约束得到严格处理。

Abstract: This article introduces a numerical optimal control framework for minmax constrained density control for a class of noisy linear parabolic partial differential equations (PDEs), in particular the noisy heat equation. The goal is to transport an initial density to a target density while minimizing a specified cost with respect to control actions and maximizing it with respect to disturbances, all within a fixed time horizon while satisfying given convex path constraints. To address this, the spatial derivatives in the PDE are discretized using finite-difference approximations, transforming the problem into a system of ordinary differential equations in time. The admissible space of control and disturbance trajectories is then finitely parametrized, and the resulting optimal control problem is formulated as a convex semi-infinite program (SIP) under mild assumptions. By leveraging new numerical tools from convex SIP theory, we establish guarantees for exact solutions that account for constraint satisfaction under an infinite family of disturbance realizations, and we establish an optimization-based computationally efficient algorithm to recover these solutions. Comprehensive numerical examples to demonstrate and validate our findings are included.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Scheduling and Routing with Degradation-Triggered Job Arrivals: An Application to Forest Firefighting with an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Fleet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18140)
*Erdi Dasdemir,Esther Jose,Rajan Batta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文定义了交织的调度与路由问题，其中新任务因现有任务的服务状态恶化而动态产生。问题以最大化总剩余奖励为目标，并以空中森林消防为背景展开，提出混合整数规划模型和基于动态约束生成的混合模型以提升可扩展性，通过计算实验和案例研究验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中（如森林火灾），任务会因延误而恶化并触发新任务的出现，这与传统假设静态任务集的调度路由问题不同。作者以"小洞不补、大洞吃苦"为核心理念（对应西方谚语'a stitch in time saves nine'），旨在建模这种任务间相互依赖、价值随时间递减的动态调度路由问题。

Method: 提出混合整数规划（MIP）模型用于最大化着火区域的保留价值；针对模型可扩展性问题，开发了一种基于动态约束生成的混合模型，通过迭代添加约束来增强求解效率。

Result: 通过计算实验和案例研究验证了模型的有效性和实际可行性，并开源代码库以保证研究可复现性。

Conclusion: 该研究为解决具有级联触发机制和动态时间窗的调度路由问题提供了建模框架，在森林消防等领域具有实际应用价值，同时通过开源代码推动后续研究。

Abstract: We define an intertwined scheduling and routing problem where new jobs appear due to the degradation of the existing jobs. Specifically, once a job arrives at a potential job location, a time window begins during which the demand of the job can be fulfilled. The demand degrades within the time window, and once it surpasses a particular threshold, it triggers the arrival of new jobs. Each job location inherently possesses an initial default reward, and the presence of an unprocessed job at a location gradually reduces this default value. The overall objective is to maximize the total remaining reward. The underlying motivation of this problem aligns with the proverb ``a stitch in time saves nine," and the problem itself carries practical implications. We focus on the problem in the context of aerial forest firefighting. Each ignited area has a designated action window; delaying intervention causes the fire to grow, diminishing the area's value and causing it to spread to adjacent areas. We develop a mixed-integer programming model that maximizes value retention in wildfire-threatened regions, and a hybrid model based on dynamic constraint generation to enhance the scalability of the model. We evaluate the performance and practicality of our models through computational experiments and a case study. Additionally, we ensure the study's reproducibility and encourage further research by providing open access to the codebase of our model.

</details>


### [104] [Trust as a Field: A Macroscopic Representation for Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18178)
*Md Mahmudul Islam,Shaurya Agarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时空信任场框架，将微观车辆级信任聚合成道路段上的连续表示，并研究了从稀疏路侧单元测量中重建完整信任场的问题，比较了坐标基深度学习基线与现场信息深度学习方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信任评估方法主要工作在单车层面，难以推理跨路段的信任演变，缺乏对信任在空间和时间上连续分布的建模能力。

Method: 提出时空信任场框架，将微观车辆级信任聚合成道路段上的连续时空表示；通过合成轨迹的受控仿真实验分析信任场行为；研究从稀疏路侧单元(RSU)测量重建完整信任场，比较坐标基深度学习基线与将信任作为车辆携带的潜变量并通过聚合机制强制测量一致性的现场信息深度学习方法。

Result: 现场信息深度学习方法能更准确地恢复轨迹对齐的低信任模式，并获得了更好的重建误差。

Conclusion: 时空信任场框架为协同互联车辆系统中的信任推理提供了新视角，且结合领域知识的深度学习方案在信任场重建任务上优于通用坐标基方法。

Abstract: Trust assessment is a fundamental component of cooperative and connected vehicle systems. However, existing approaches operate primarily at the level of individual vehicles, making it difficult to reason about trust evolution across road segments. In this paper, we propose a spatio-temporal trust-field framework that aggregates microscopic vehicle-level trust into a continuous representation over space and time. The trust field is formally defined on road segments. We conducted simulation-based experiments using synthetic trajectories generated under controlled conditions, enabling analysis of trust-field behavior in simple road scenarios. Beyond theoretical modeling, we study an implication of the trust-field concept: reconstructing the full trust field from sparse roadside-unit (RSU) measurements. We compare (i) a coordinate-based deep learning baseline that learns a generic trust field from sparse samples and (ii) a field-informed deep learning method that treats trust as a latent quantity carried by vehicles and enforces measurement consistency through the aggregation mechanism. The field-informed approach more accurately recovers trajectory-aligned low-trust patterns and yields improved reconstruction error.

</details>


### [105] [Revisiting the "Push-T" Robot Manipulation Task with Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18227)
*Shuangyu Xie,Kaiyuan Chen,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了经典的Push-T基准任务，利用LLM编码智能体（Claude Code与Fable 5）通过代理式编码循环生成无需任何人类演示数据的算法化策略。该智能体在网上找到2D模拟环境，通过模拟实验学习推动机制并迭代优化，以比200个演示训练的扩散策略少46%的步骤实现了100%成功率，并进一步通过自生成课程扩展到Push-A到Push-Z全套字母任务，以及Franka和UR5机器人在3D跨实体模拟中的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者重新审视Push-T这一经典模仿学习基准，探究新兴的Agentic Robotics范式下，LLM编码智能体能否通过自主编码与模拟实验的方式，完全摆脱对人类演示数据的依赖，生成有效的操作策略。

Method: 使用Claude Code编码智能体与Fable 5模型，通过代理式编码循环让智能体在线搜索2D模拟环境，自主进行模拟实验学习推动力学，迭代优化代码策略。智能体还通过自生成课程将任务从T形块扩展到全部26个字母，并生成Franka和UR5机械臂的3D跨实体仿真代码。

Result: 智能体生成的代码策略达到100%成功率，比使用200个人类演示训练的最佳扩散策略少46%的步骤；成功扩展到Push-A至Push-Z全字母任务，并生成Franka和UR5机械臂的3D跨实体仿真代码。

Conclusion: LLM编码智能体在无需演示数据的情况下，能够通过代理式编码循环和模拟实验解决经典操作任务，并在效率和泛化能力上超越基于模仿学习的基线方法，展示了Agentic Robotics范式的潜力。

Abstract: Push-T is an iconic benchmark for learning manipulation policies from human demonstrations. The robot must use a single point of contact to push a T-shaped block into a target pose. In this short paper, we revisit the Push-T task in the context of emerging advances in Agentic Robotics where an LLM coding agent -- Claude Code with Fable 5 -- is prompted to create an algorithmic solution that does not require any demonstration data. We study how effective the agentic coding loop can solve the Push-T task, and compare the resulting code as policy with the visuomotor imitation learning policy. Results suggest that the agent found the 2D gym simulation online, and used sim experiments to learn push mechanics, iteratively optimizing to achieve 100% success rate using 46% fewer steps than the best diffusion policy trained with 200 human demonstrations. The coding agent also solve extensions from T to the full alphabet (Push-A to Push-Z) using a self generated curriculum and generated simulation code for the Franka and UR5 robot arms in 3D cross-embodiment simulations with visual feedback. Videos, policies and details will be posted online.

</details>


### [106] [GigaBrain-WBC-0.5: A Behavior World Model for Robust Whole-Body Control with Environment Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18234)
*Ziyang Cheng,Tianshu Tang,Jinxin Lan,Xinze Chen,Yuhan Gong,Zhichao Liu,Changzhong Wu,Yahao Mao,Zongyan Deng,Mingxuan Ma,Huasen Xi,Yilong Liu,Yutong Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Yang Wang,Yun Ye,Guan Huang,Xiaojie Jin,Zheng Zhu,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GigaBrain-WBC-0.5，首个用于人形机器人全身控制的行为世界模型（BWM）。该模型训练一个因果Transformer联合预测下一动作、下一状态和下一潜在行为命令的分布，使执行策略同时建模环境如何塑造可行行为。通过自动地形标注管线恢复3D接触几何，在线检测不合理的命令并回退到学习到的行为，实现"尽力而为"的任务执行。在四个场景中取得最高成功率：地形交互81.3%（4.3倍于最强基线），不合理命令下83.1%，跌倒恢复99.3%（16.8倍于最强基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身运动跟踪策略仅在平坦地面有效，因在空场景中训练而无法学习地形和物体接触如何重塑动力学。试图通过无限扩大参考运动语料来教会策略在任意命令下保持平衡的方法，在可行行为依赖于环境时失效。需要一个能够建模环境影响的全身控制策略。

Method: 1) 训练因果Transformer同时预测下一动作、下一状态和下一潜在行为命令的分布，将世界模型与策略结合；2) 开发自动地形标注管线，从重定向运动恢复完整3D接触几何，实现与现有运动数据集规模相匹配的地形标注；3) 在部署时重用预测分布来在线检测不合理命令并回退到学习到的行为。

Result: 在四个场景中均取得最高成功率：地形交互81.3%（最强基线的4.3倍），不合理命令下83.1%，跌倒恢复99.3%（最强基线的16.8倍）。硬件实验显示在缺失支撑和扰动下具有鲁棒交互能力；Unitree G1检查点通过简单微调迁移到Maker L01机器人。

Conclusion: 行为世界模型（BWM）作为统一策略，能同时实现实时命令响应、环境交互和对不合理命令、跌倒及扰动的鲁棒性。该方法的有效性来自"执行者即建模者"的设计理念——通过让行动网络同时预测环境如何塑造下一步行为，而非依赖无限扩充参考运动数据，从根本上解决了现有跟踪器在环境依赖场景中的局限。

Abstract: Whole-body motion tracking policies turn a humanoid into a robust control interface: the teleoperator---or an upstream model---only supplies a coarse movement intent, while the low-level policy keeps the robot balanced and physically feasible. Existing trackers deliver this interface only on flat ground: trained in empty scenes, they never learn how contact with terrain and objects reshapes their dynamics, and they attempt to teach the policy to balance under any command by continually enlarging the reference-motion corpus, which stops working once feasible behaviors become environment-dependent. We present GigaBrain-WBC-0.5, the first Behavior World Model (BWM) for humanoid whole-body control. Rather than a purely reactive tracker, we train a causal Transformer to jointly predict its next action, next state, and the distribution over its next latent behavior command, so the network that acts also models how the environment shapes what it can do next. An automatic terrain-annotation pipeline recovers full 3D contact geometry from retargeted motion, enabling terrain annotation at the scale of existing motion datasets. The predicted distribution is reused at deployment to detect implausible commands online and retract them onto learned behaviors, so the robot attempts tasks in a "best-effort" manner. The result is a unified policy that takes real-time command, interacts with environment, and stays robust to implausible commands, falls, and disturbances. GigaBrain-WBC-0.5 achieves the highest success rate across all four regimes among three large-scale tracker baselines: 81.3% on terrain interaction (4.3x the strongest baseline), 83.1% under implausible commands, and 99.3% fall recovery (16.8x the strongest baseline). Hardware trials show robust interaction under missing supports and disturbances; the Unitree G1 checkpoint transfers to the Maker L01 robot with simple fine-tuning.

</details>


### [107] [GAPL: Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning for LLM-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18254)
*Zhihong Cui,Hengyu Liu,Zhangkai Wu,Yushuai Li,Tianyi Li,Peiyuan Guan,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAPL是一个将LLM效果估计、仿真效果验证和策略优化整合为闭环系统的自动驾驶轨迹规划框架，在四个Highway-env场景中显著优于基线方法，分别降低了碰撞率0.76、平均位移误差0.86和最终位移误差2.00，并提升了1.44的平均奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶轨迹规划需要高层推理与精准低层控制相结合。虽然LLM具备语义丰富的规划能力，但其应用受限于幻觉推理、对环境动态的认知不足以及数值控制精度有限等问题。

Method: GAPL由三个模块组成：(1)基于LLM的效果评估器，用于结构化多维动作效果估计；(2)基于仿真的效果校正器，通过模拟器滚动预测与动力学一致的效果；(3)效果感知决策器，利用蒸馏器将LLM效果估计与仿真结果对齐，引导基于PPO的策略学习。

Result: 在四个Highway-env场景中，GAPL一致性地优于所有基线方法。具体而言，碰撞率平均降低0.76，平均位移误差(ADE)降低0.86，最终位移误差(FDE)降低2.00，平均奖励提升1.44。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的语义推理能力与仿真环境的动力学一致性约束相结合，GAPL有效解决了LLM在轨迹规划中的幻觉与精度不足问题，展示了LLM与物理仿真融合在自动驾驶规划中的可行性。

Abstract: Trajectory planning for autonomous driving requires both high-level reasoning and precise low-level control. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer semantic-rich planning capabilities, however, their application is limited by hallucinated reasoning, poor grounding in environment dynamics, and limited numerical precision in control. We propose GAPL (Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning), a unified framework that integrates LLM-based effect estimation, simulation-based effect grounding, and policy optimization into a closed-loop system. GAPL consists of three modules: (1) an LLM-based Effect Evaluator for structured multi-dimensional action-effect estimation; (2) a Simulation-based Effect Grounder that predicts dynamics-consistent effects from simulator rollouts; and (3) an Effect-Aware Decision Maker that grounds LLM effect estimates against simulation via a distiller to guide Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based policy learning. Experiments on four Highway-env scenarios demonstrate that GAPL consistently outperforms baselines, achieving average reductions of {0.76, 0.86, 2.00} in collision rate, average displacement error (ADE), and final displacement error (FDE), and an average reward gain of 1.44.

</details>


### [108] [VERAGMIL: Virtual Environment for Scooping Granular Foods with Imitation Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18258)
*Amanuel Ergogo,Diego Dall'Alba,Przemyslaw Korzeniowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VERAGMIL框架，结合高保真模拟器与虚拟现实（VR）界面，用于收集演示数据以训练机器人辅助喂食（RAF）系统处理颗粒状食物。评估三种模仿学习模型（BC、BC-RNN、BCQ）在颗粒舀取和搬运任务中的表现，结果显示VR演示显著优于3D空间鼠标，BCQ模型在减少溢出和接近人类水平方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助喂食系统对帮助残疾或运动障碍人群至关重要，但处理米粒、豆类等颗粒状食物因物理特性动态变化而极具挑战。从人类演示中学习虽有前景，但获取高质量演示数据十分困难，亟需一种有效的数据采集与训练框架。

Method: VERAGMIL框架整合了高保真模拟器与直觉化VR界面，提供包含机器人、传感器及具有不同物理特性的多种食物模型的真实环境。作者在颗粒舀取和搬运任务中训练三种模仿学习模型（BC、BC-RNN、BCQ），分别使用VR界面和3D空间鼠标采集演示数据，并以人类专家表现为基线进行比较。

Result: VR界面采集的演示数据显著优于3D空间鼠标数据；BCQ模型在所有模型中整体表现最佳，尤其在减少食物溢出方面最为突出，整体性能接近人类专家水平。模型在成功率、溢出量、对未见食物泛化能力及任务完成时间等指标上均被系统评估。

Conclusion: VERAGMIL框架有效支持了RAF系统在颗粒状食材处理中的模仿学习训练，验证了VR作为一种高质量演示采集手段的可行性。框架代码已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Robot-Assisted Feeding (RAF) systems are essential for assisting individuals with disabilities or motor impairments in eating tasks. Manipulating granular food items, such as rice and beans, poses significant challenges due to their dynamic physical properties. Learning from human demonstrations offers a promising solution, but acquiring high-quality demonstrations is complex. To address this, we present VERAGMIL, a framework that combines a high-fidelity simulator with an intuitive Virtual Reality (VR) interface for recording demonstrations and supporting different imitation learning methods. VERAGMIL provides a realistic environment for training RAF systems to handle granular materials, including robots, sensors, and various food items with distinct physical characteristics. We evaluate VERAGMIL by training three imitation learning models, BC, BC-RNN, and BCQ, on granular scooping and transporting tasks using both VR interface and 3D space mouse demonstrations, comparing them with a human-expert baseline. The models are assessed on success rate, spillage, generalization to unseen food items, and task completion time. Results show that VR-based demonstrations significantly outperform 3D space mouse data, with BCQ achieving the best overall performance, particularly in reducing spillage and approaching human performance. These findings underscore the effectiveness of our framework for training RAF systems in granular material handling. The code for our framework is publicly available at: https://github.com/AmanuelErgogo/VERAGMIL.git.

</details>


### [109] [Transferable Tool-Tissue Contact Detection from Stereo Depth in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18270)
*Mingyeung Wu,Zhonghao Zhang,Hao Yang,Alan Kuntz,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用深度图像中工具-组织间的最小距离信号（-log10|d|）进行机器人辅助手术中的工具-组织接触检测。通过全监督两状态隐马尔可夫模型，在六折留一会话（LOSO）集成上训练，在跨任务、跨模型的泛化实验中达到宏F1 0.927、AUPRC 0.980，显著优于基于RGB外观的接触分类器（宏F1仅0.320），证明工具-组织距离是强泛化能力的接触检测线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具-组织接触检测方法大多基于RGB外观学习接触分类器，泛化能力差（面对不同任务或不同组织模型时性能大幅下降）。需要一种不依赖外观、可跨场景迁移的接触检测信号，以支持交互感知控制和下游力估计。

Method: 利用立体相机生成的深度图像，在每帧中围绕工具边界定位空间支持的最小距离块，将其约简为单一标量-log10|d|，该信号随真实接触状态同步升降。将该观测形式化为全监督的两状态隐马尔可夫模型（HMM），在六个触诊会话上以六折留一会话（LOSO）方式集成训练，决策阈值从池化的折外预测中选择。

Result: 在四个留出会话（涵盖同任务同模型、同任务不同模型、不同任务不同模型三种类别）上，该模型达到宏F1 0.927、AUPRC 0.980。相比之下，基于RGB的接触分类器在同一任务同一模型类别上F1达0.965，但在其他两类上性能大幅下降，四会话整体宏F1仅0.320。

Conclusion: 工具-组织距离是接触检测中强大且可迁移的线索。基于深度距离信号的HMM方法在跨任务、跨模型场景下具有显著优于RGB外观方法的泛化能力，为机器人辅助手术中的接触检测提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable tool--tissue contact detection can support interaction-aware control and downstream force estimation in robot-assisted surgery. Most existing methods learn a contact classifier from RGB appearance, which is hard to generalize. In this work, we use the depth image generated from a stereo pair to give more information about tool--tissue contact. For each depth frame, we localize a spatially supported minimum-distance patch around the tool boundary and reduce it to a single scalar, $-\log_{10}|d|$; this signal rises and falls in step with ground-truth contact. We formalize this observation with a fully supervised two-state hidden Markov model. We fit this model as a six-fold leave-one-session-out (LOSO) ensemble on six palpation sessions against a single silicone cup-like phantom, with the decision threshold selected from the pooled out-of-fold predictions. It is evaluated on four held-out sessions of three categories: 1. same task on same phantom; 2. same task on different phantom; 3. different task on different phantom. This model reaches held-out macro F1 $0.927$ and AUPRC $0.980$. We further compare against a reproduction of an RGB-based contact classifier from prior work. This RGB-based model achieves high performance on the first category (F1 $0.965$), but substantially lower performance on the other two, resulting in macro F1 $0.320$ across all four sessions. These results indicate that the tool--tissue distance is a strong, transferable cue for contact detection in robot-assisted surgery.

</details>


### [110] [GuideFetch: A Task Coordination Framework for Concurrent Navigation and Object Retrieval in Assistive Robot Dogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18292)
*Qian Yin,Ruiping Liu,Kunyu Peng,Jianxiang Man,Isik Baran Sandan,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Di Wen,Kailun Yang,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GuideFetch，一个用于异构引导者与取物者机器人团队并发导航与物体抓取的协调框架。LLM从自然语言指令实例化调度条件化的四动作模式，在执行前对机器人、技能和目标别名进行归一化，并对动作进行验证。在90种场景与种子组合（360次执行）的2×2匹配研究中，所有180次在线LLM响应均无需回退或重放即可通过验证，并发执行相比顺序执行在共同完成的56个案例中平均缩短41.3%的Makespan。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导盲犬引导盲人入座，同时另一台辅助机器狗取咖啡并送至同一座位的场景，需要并发执行导航与物体抓取任务。然而，语法上有效的LLM计划可能违反具身约束（如机器人能力或目标位置限制），且控制器运动成功并不等同于任务真正完成。因此需要一个协调框架来区分计划有效性与任务完成验证。

Method: GuideFetch框架包含以下核心组件：1）LLM从自然语言指令实例化调度条件化的四动作模式（schedule-conditioned four-action schema）；2）执行前对机器人、技能和目标别名进行归一化处理；3）针对注册目标、机器人能力及所选调度方案对提议动作进行验证；4）根据机器人与物体状态决定顺序或并行执行。

Result: 在90种场景与种子组合的2×2匹配研究中，总共执行360次。所有180次在线LLM响应均通过验证，无需回退或重放，且与对应的脚本化计划完全匹配。对于每种规划源，顺序执行和并行执行分别达到72/90和71/90的操作成功率。在两种调度均完成的56个案例中，并行执行平均缩短makespan达41.3%。

Conclusion: 在该受控设置中，角色专业化与动作重叠能够缩短完成任务的时间，同时状态检查能够有效区分计划有效性与已验证的任务完成。这表明GuideFetch框架在并发异构机器人任务协调中的有效性与可行性。

Abstract: Consider a robot guide dog escorting a blind user to an available seat while a second assistive robot dog concurrently retrieves a cup of coffee and delivers it to the same seat. This setting motivates concurrent execution because navigation and object retrieval can overlap. A syntactically valid Large Language Model (LLM) plan may still violate embodiment constraints, and successful-looking controller motion does not by itself establish task completion. We introduce \textsc{GuideFetch}, a coordination framework for concurrent navigation and object retrieval by a heterogeneous guider and fetcher team. An LLM instantiates a schedule-conditioned four-action schema from a natural-language instruction. Before execution, robot, skill, and target aliases are normalized, and proposed actions are validated against registered targets, robot capabilities, and the selected schedule. Robot and object states then govern sequential and parallel execution. In a matched $2\times2$ study across 90 combinations of scene and seed (360 executions), all 180 online LLM responses validate without fallback or replay and match the corresponding scripted plans. For each planner source, sequential and parallel execution achieve $72/90$ and $71/90$ operational successes, respectively. Among the 56 cases completed by both schedules, parallel execution reduces mean makespan by 41.3\%. Within this controlled setting, role specialization and action overlap shorten completed missions, while state checks distinguish plan validity from verified mission completion. Source code will be available.

</details>


### [111] [A Task-Agnostic Control Strategy for Dynamic Assistance with Pneumatically Actuated Soft Exosuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18364)
*Anoush Sepehri,Zachary Huang,Raymond de Callafon,Michael T. Tolley,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于逆对象控制的策略，用于气动软体外骨骼，仅依赖运动学感知即可实现任务无关的动态辅助，通过Hammerstein模型（Preisach迟滞模型+线性时不变滤波器）建模人机系统，实验显示交互力矩降低73%，关键肌肉激活降低47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动人工肌肉为上肢软体外骨骼提供了新的可能性，但其复杂的动力学和有限带宽使得基于用户意图的响应性辅助成为长期挑战。现有方法通常需要生理或力传感器来解读用户意图，增加了系统复杂性和成本。

Method: 采用Hammerstein动态模型（由Preisach迟滞模型和线性时不变滤波器组成）对气动软体外骨骼的人机系统进行建模，利用140秒数据对每位用户进行模型个性化，并近似求解逆模型集成到控制回路中，实现仅依靠运动学感知的任务无关动态辅助。

Result: 在模拟腕部软体辅助外骨骼的测试平台上，与无辅助条件相比，该控制器在8°/s到120°/s的速度范围内将交互力矩降低了最高73%，关键屈肌和伸肌的激活降低了最高47%。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种无需生理或力传感器即可解读用户意图的、可提供任务无关动态辅助的气动软体外骨骼控制策略，为日常生活中的气动软体外骨骼应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Pneumatic artificial muscles have provided new opportunities to develop upper-extremity soft exosuits for reha- bilitation, augmentation, and assisted daily living. However, the complex dynamics and limited bandwidth of these actuators has made providing responsive assistance based on user intention a longstanding challenge. In this work, we present an inverse-plant control strategy for pneumatically actuated soft exosuits that only relies on kinematic sensing for task-agnostic and dynamic assistance during daily living. We model the human-robot system using a Hammerstein dynamic model, consisting of a Preisach hysteresis model and a linear time-invariant filter, to capture the static and dynamic behavior of the system. We personalize our model to each user using 140 s of data and approximate an inverse to integrate into our control loop. When evaluated on a test rig that emulated a soft assistive exosuit for the wrist, our controller reduced the interaction torque by up to 73% and the activation of key flexor and extensor muscles by up to 47% relative to the condition with no assistance for speeds ranging from 8°/s to 120°/s. Overall, this work presents a control strategy that can provide task-agnostic, dynamic assistance with pneumatically actuated soft exosuits without the need for physiological or force sensors to interpret user intention.

</details>


### [112] [The Embodiment Gap in Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18433)
*Yukiyasu Domae,Keisuke Shirai,Hanbit Oh,Ryoichi Nakajo,Tomohiro Motoda,Koshi Makihara,Masaki Murooka,Takuma Yagi,Yoshiaki Bando,Ryo Hanai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了"具身差距"（embodiment gap）这一概念，指可复用模型、表征或数据与在目标机器人上实际执行之间的差距。文章通过综述现有跨具身（cross-embodiment）工作，提出了一个二维图谱来定位不同方法的共享结构和适应阶段，并给出了一个超越成功率、明确适应工作内容的报告框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型（RFMs）常被从规模化视角讨论（更多数据、更大模型、更广基准），但忽略了模型即使泛化良好，仍需要在特定机器人本体上进行额外适配工作才能执行。不同方法和目标机器人所需的适配工作量不同，直接影响实际部署，现有文献缺乏对这一差距的系统性分析。

Method: 作者首先定义"具身差距"概念，然后构建一个二维图谱：一轴表示共享结构的类型（如语义/感知、数据/接口、跨具身对应关系），另一轴表示执行前需要进行适配的阶段。随后综述近期工作并按三个重叠研究方向分类：语义与感知共享、机器人数据与接口共享、跨具身对应的学习。最后提出一个适应工作报告框架，要求报告超越成功率之外的工作量信息。

Result: 文章提供了一个系统化的跨具身学习综述框架，识别出当前方法在共享结构类型和适配阶段上的分布，并提出了一个新的报告框架，用以评估在新机器人上仍需完成的工作量，弥补了仅以成功率作为评价标准的不足。

Conclusion: 具身差距是评估机器人基础模型实用性的关键维度，成功率高不等于部署成本低。未来研究应重视跨具身学习中的适应工作量，并通过标准化的报告框架使该工作量可量化、可对比。

Abstract: Robot foundation models (RFMs), including vision-language-action (VLA) policies, are often discussed through a scaling view: more data, larger models, and broader benchmarks should improve generalization. In robotics, however, a model can generalize while work still remains before it can run on a robot with a particular body. The work required differs across methods and target robots, and those differences affect practical deployment. We call the gap between reusable models, representations, or data and their use in execution on the target robot the embodiment gap. This survey examines what can be reused across robot embodiments and what must still be implemented on a new robot. We place existing methods on a two-axis map that shows the type of shared structure and the stage at which adaptation is needed for execution on the target robot. We then examine recent work through three overlapping research directions: sharing semantics and perception, sharing robot data and interfaces, and learning correspondence across embodiments. We also propose a reporting framework for adaptation work that success rate alone does not reveal. The framework identifies the work that should be checked when comparing cross-embodiment learning and highlights work that remains on a new robot and questions for future study.

</details>


### [113] [HarvestPoint-ACT: Explicit Target Selection and Harvest-Point Conditioning for Robotic Fruit Harvesting under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18446)
*Hanying Hu,Weipeng Li,Yikun Huang,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HarvestPoint-ACT系统，通过实例分割与关键点分支显式预测每个可见水果的采摘点，并结合调度器按遮挡程度和行程距离排序选择目标，利用八维状态编码驱动动作分块Transformer进行仿人采摘，在遮挡环境下达到88%成功率（重度遮挡下75%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端模仿学习虽避免了手工设计抓取动作，但策略仍需自行决定采摘哪个水果以及在何处闭合夹爪。遮挡会导致策略在采摘过程中丢失目标水果，且仅从像素推断正确的闭合点较为困难，因此需要将这两个决策在感知层面显式化。

Method: 构建HarvestPoint-ACT：1）实例分割前端配合关键点分支，为每个可见水果输出掩码和采摘点；2）调度器按遮挡程度与行程距离对候选目标排序并选择一个目标，每次尝试后重新检测与重排序以应对树冠变化；3）将选中水果编码为八维状态（包括绝对采摘点、夹爪到该点的向量、有效性标志和置信度）输入动作分块Transformer；4）目标暂未被检测到时，保留机器人基坐标系下最近一次采摘点估计并标记为过期，若持续丢失则中止尝试。

Result: 在树冠模拟装置上，HarvestPoint-ACT达到88%的成功率，在重度遮挡条件下仍有75%的成功率。

Conclusion: 将目标选择和采摘点确定从隐式策略决策中解耦出来，通过显式的感知表征与调度机制，能显著提升遮挡环境下机器人采摘的成功率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: End-to-end imitation learning avoids hand-made robot motion for approaching and grasping, but the policy must still decide which fruit to pick and where to close the gripper. Occlusion can make the policy lose the selected fruit during harvesting, and the correct closing point is difficult to infer from pixels alone. This paper presents HarvestPoint-ACT, which makes both decisions explicit in perception and provides them to the policy. An instance segmentation front end with a keypoint branch predicts a mask and a harvest point for each visible fruit, where the harvest point specifies the location to close the gripper. A scheduler ranks detected candidates by occlusion and travel distance and selects one target. After each attempt, it redetects and reranks the candidates because the canopy may have changed. The selected fruit is encoded for an action chunking transformer as an eight-dimensional state, containing the absolute harvest point, the vector from the gripper to that point, a validity flag, and a confidence score. When the selected fruit is temporarily undetected, the system retains the last harvest point estimate in the robot base frame and marks it as stale, and aborts the attempt if the loss persists. On a canopy mock-up, HarvestPoint-ACT achieves a success rate of 88%, and of 75% under heavy occlusion.

</details>


### [114] [Backward Layout Search for Sequence-Constrained Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18454)
*Xi Zhang,Jiancong Dai,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BLS（反向布局搜索）的机器人装配布局规划方法。基于"每个装配步骤的可行性仅取决于当前及后续装配零件的初始位姿"这一关键观察，BLS按逆装配顺序为零件分配初始位姿，通过几何、运动学、抓取和规定运动检查逐层筛选，结合碰撞掩码和候选集过滤剔除不可行候选，并用束搜索保留有前景的部分布局，最终以前向装配顺序的完整运动规划验证布局。在五个装配模型上的实验表明，BLS能够生成无碰撞可执行的布局，并减少步骤评估次数和搜索时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配布局规划需要同时确定装配位置和每个零件的初始位姿，同时保证规定装配序列的无碰撞执行。该问题具有挑战性：每完成一个装配步骤，障碍物环境都会改变，工作空间中尚未装配的零件可能会阻挡机器人运动。现有方法难以高效处理这种动态变化的障碍物环境。

Method: 作者观察到每个装配步骤的可行性仅取决于当前及后续装配零件的初始位姿，基于此提出BLS算法：按逆装配顺序分配零件初始位姿；每次扩展执行几何、运动学、抓取和规定运动检查；通过碰撞掩码和候选集过滤去除不可行的初始位姿候选；使用束搜索保留有前景的部分布局；完整布局通过前向装配顺序的全运动规划进行验证。

Result: 在五个装配模型上的实验表明，BLS能生成无碰撞可执行的装配布局，且与匹配的前向搜索相比，显著减少了步骤评估次数和搜索时间。

Conclusion: BLS利用装配步骤可行性依赖性的结构特征，通过反向搜索策略有效解决了动态障碍物环境下的装配布局规划问题，在效率和可行性上均优于前向搜索方法。

Abstract: Robotic assembly layout planning must determine the assembly site and the initial pose of each part while ensuring collision-free execution of a prescribed assembly sequence. This problem is challenging because the obstacle environment changes after each assembly step, and unassembled parts re maining in the workspace may block robot motions. We observe that the feasibility of each assembly step depends only on the initial poses of the current and later-assembled parts. Based on this dependency, we propose Backward Layout Search (BLS), which assigns initial part poses in reverse assembly order. Each expansion performs geometric, kinematic, grasp, and prescribed-motion checks, while collision masks and candidate set filtering remove infeasible initial part pose candidates. Promising partial layouts are retained through beam selection, and complete layouts are validated by full motion planning in forward assembly order. Experiments on five assembly models show that BLS produces collision-free executable layouts and reduces step evaluations and search time compared with a matched forward search.

</details>


### [115] [DevGRU: Depth-guided Visual Navigation using a Collision-aware Recurrent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18470)
*Kyung Min Han,Eunsom Kim,Young J. Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DevGRU，一种基于深度图像和点目标条件的导航系统，通过动作预测器生成碰撞感知的未来轨迹，并配合碰撞预测器补偿目标位姿估计误差，在复杂室内环境中显著优于ViNT、NoMaD和NavDP等基线方法，同时拥有更小的模型参数量和更快的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航模型旨在开发跨平台泛化的基础模型，但在复杂室内环境（尤其是结构布局和狭窄通道）中容易发生碰撞，亟需一种能有效避免碰撞并补偿目标位姿累积误差的导航系统。

Method: DevGRU采用深度图像和点目标作为条件输入，核心组件包括：(1) 动作预测器（AP），生成碰撞感知的未来轨迹以规避即时障碍；(2) 碰撞预测器，与AP协同补偿目标位姿估计中累积的误差，并主动缓解未来偏差。

Result: 在九个不同场景中与ViNT、NoMaD、NavDP及四个ViNT/NoMaD变体进行对比实验，DevGRU在导航性能上大幅超越ViNT和NoMaD；模型参数量比NavDP小7倍，推理速度快17倍，推理时间在基線中最快。

Conclusion: DevGRU通过碰撞感知动作预测器有效解决了现有导航模型在复杂室内环境中的碰撞问题，同时以轻量级设计实现了性能与效率的兼顾，展示了在嵌入式平台上部署的潜力。

Abstract: Existing visual navigation models often aim to develop foundation models that can generalize robot navigation across diverse platforms. However, many of these models are prone to collisions when deployed in complex indoor environments, particularly in structured layouts and narrow passages. To address this problem, we propose a depth image- and point-goal-conditioned navigation system, DevGRU. The proposed system employs an action predictor (AP) that generates collision-aware future trajectories, enabling effective avoidance of immediate obstacles. In conjunction with a collision predictor, the AP further compensates for errors accumulated in the goal pose estimation and proactively mitigates future deviations. To evaluate our method, we conducted experiments across nine different scenes and three state-of-the-art approaches - ViNT, NoMaD, and NavDP - as well as four additional variants of ViNT and NoMaD. In terms of navigation performance, DevGRU significantly outperforms ViNT and NoMaD by a large margin. In addition, the proposed model has a relatively small number of trainable parameters, resulting in the fastest inference time among the baselines, particularly outperforming NavDP by 7x in model size and 17x in inference time.

</details>


### [116] [An Experimental Study of Downwash Effects on a Continuum Manipulator Integrated with a Multirotor UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18507)
*Anuraj Uthayasooriyan,Krishna Manaswi Digumarti,ernando Vanegas,Felipe Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了旋翼无人机下洗气流对腱驱动连续体机械臂（CM）末端位姿的扰动影响，并提出了基于常曲率引导的高斯过程回归（GPR）残差模型，可将位姿预测RMSE降低（位置89-95%，姿态47-79%），支持下洗感知的连续体臂空中操控建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体臂空中操控系统利用软机械臂的柔顺性和灵活性在受限或危险环境中作业，但螺旋桨下洗气流会劣化性能，尤其在近壁面和近地面场景。然而，下洗效应对连续体机械臂的影响此前尚未被表征，缺乏对其扰动规律的定量理解。

Method: 实验方法：(1)在静风条件下将连续体机械臂与常曲率（CC）模型对比验证基线；(2)在四种桨叶油门水平下于自由空间量化下洗引起的末端位姿偏差，并在最大油门条件下测试近壁面和近地面场景；(3)基于实验数据学习CC引导的GPR残差模型，用于补偿末端位姿预测偏差。

Result: 研究发现：下洗导致的末端位姿偏差中，垂直位置与偏航角偏差最大，且地面效应会放大这些偏差。提出的CC引导GPR残差模型显著提升了前向位姿预测精度，位置误差降低89-95%，姿态误差降低47-79%。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统表征了空中连续体机械臂在下洗气流下的运动学偏差，并验证了数据驱动残差模型的有效性，为设计补偿导向、下洗感知的连续体臂空中操控系统提供了建模基础。

Abstract: Continuum arm aerial manipulation systems leverage soft-manipulator compliance and dexterity for tasks in confined or hazardous environments, but propeller downwash can degrade performance, particularly near walls and the ground. This effect remains uncharacterized for continuum manipulators. This letter experimentally studies downwash-induced kinematic deviations of a tendon-driven continuum manipulator integrated with a multirotor platform. Under still-air conditions, the CM is compared with a constant-curvature (CC) model. Downwash- induced end-effector pose deviations are then quantified relative to the mean still-air experimental baseline at four propeller throttle levels in free space, and at maximum throttle near a wall, and near the ground. Vertical position and yaw show the largest deviations and are amplified by ground effect. A CC-guided Gaussian process regression (GPR) residual model is learned from experimental data that improves forward pose prediction RMSE (position by 89-95%, orientation by 47-79%), and support compensation-oriented, downwash-aware modeling of continuum arm aerial manipulation systems.

</details>


### [117] [Real-Time Control-Constrained DDP for Underactuated Balancing of Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18552)
*SeongWon Nam,Hyunyong Lee,Hansol Kang,Jiman Park,Yeongwoo Son,Bumsu Yi,Jaeyoung Oh,Hyouk Ryeol Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于加速投影梯度（APG）的控制约束差分动态规划（ABC-DDP）框架，用于欠驱动腿式机器人的实时模型预测控制（MPC）。该方法通过虚拟约束将控制约束整合进可行性驱动的多重打靶框架中，无需重复KKT逆运算即可高效求解约束问题并识别活动集。仿真验证了在统一MPC框架下实现静态双腿站立、慢速猫步、直立行走和高速奔跑等多种运动，首次通过实时有限时域MPC实现了四足机器人的静态双腿站立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DDP在运用于欠驱动腿式机器人时无法有效处理控制约束（如关节力矩/转矩限制），限制了其在实时MPC中的应用；同时传统约束处理方法需要重复进行KKT逆运算，计算成本高，难以满足实时性要求。

Method: 1) 提出APG-based控制约束DDP（ABC-DDP），利用加速投影梯度法高效求解约束最优控制问题并识别活动集，避免重复KKT逆运算；2) 引入虚拟约束，将控制约束整合到可行性驱动的多重打靶（multiple-shooting）框架中，保证即使从动态不可行初始状态出发也能稳定优化；3) 支持短时间内具有强欠驱动特性的实时MPC。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的统一MPC框架能够实现：静态双腿站立（抗外部扰动）、慢速猫步、直立行走以及高速奔跑等多种动态运动，涵盖静止与高速运动等多种工况；其中静态双腿站立为四足机器人通过实时有限时域MPC首次实现。

Conclusion: ABC-DDP在保持实时计算能力的同时有效处理了控制约束，使得单一统一MPC框架能够覆盖从静态站立到高速奔跑的广泛运动范围，为欠驱动腿式机器人的实时约束最优控制提供了一种高效可行的方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a real-time control-constrained Differential Dynamic Programming (DDP) framework for underactuated legged robots. To address the limitation of classical DDP in handling control constraints, we propose an Accelerated Projected Gradient (APG)-based control-constrained DDP (ABC-DDP), which efficiently computes constrained solutions and identifies active sets without repeated Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) inversions. A virtual constraint is introduced to integrate control constraints within a feasibility-driven multiple-shooting framework, enabling stable optimization even from dynamically infeasible initializations. The proposed method supports real-time model predictive control (MPC) with short horizons under strong underactuation. Simulation results demonstrate static two-leg standing under external disturbances, along with diverse dynamic motions including slow catwalk, upright walking, and high-speed running within a unified MPC framework. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of static two-leg standing of a quadruped robot achieved using real-time finite-horizon MPC.

</details>


### [118] [LabDex: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Dexterous Manipulation in Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18618)
*Zhipeng Tang,Sihang Chen,Sha Zhang,Peihao Yang,Yan Liu,Wentao Zhao,Xinrui Liu,Rui Huang,Wensheng Du,Yuting Huang,Jiajun Deng,Lidian Wang,Yuan Zhang,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LabDex是一个大规模真实世界数据集与基准，用于化学实验室中的灵巧操作，通过层次化任务分类（原子技能、组合任务、长时程实验）统一了真实世界与仿真平台，支持跨层级的机器人学习策略训练与系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主实验室有望加速科学发现，但现有基准未能同时涵盖灵巧手部使用、真实实验室交互和多阶段实验流程，缺乏对机器人实验室灵巧操作能力的系统性训练与评估框架。

Method: LabDex采用层次化任务分类法，将化学实验室操作系统地组织为三个互联层级：原子技能（刻画基础灵巧操作能力）、组合技能、以及长时程实验室工作流。该数据集为跨平台设计，首次在统一框架下整合真实世界与仿真平台，提供标准化的任务定义、演示数据和评估协议。研究者在真实与仿真环境中对代表性机器人学习方法进行了跨层级评估。

Result: 跨层级实验验证了LabDex任务设计与演示数据的有效性，表明该基准能够支持现有机器人策略在实验室灵巧操作任务中不同层级的训练和系统评估。

Conclusion: LabDex为自主实验室机器人的研究与发展提供了基础性支撑，其层次化组织结构不仅支持端任务性能评估，还使研究者能够分析基础灵巧技能如何组合并影响更复杂的实验室操作。

Abstract: Autonomous laboratories hold great promise for accelerating scientific discovery. To achieve this vision, robots are supposed to dexterously manipulate diverse labware and instruments and execute long-horizon, state-dependent experimental procedures. Yet existing benchmarks do not jointly capture dexterous hand use, real-world laboratory interactions, and multi-stage experimental procedures, limiting systematic training and evaluation. To bridge this gap, we introduce LabDex, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for dexterous manipulation in chemistry laboratories, organized around a hierarchical task taxonomy spanning atomic skills, compositional tasks, and long-horizon experiments. First, LabDex is cross-platform and, for the first time, unifies real-world and simulation platforms under a common framework, providing standardized task definitions, demonstrations, and evaluation protocols. Second, LabDex is large-scale and systematically organizes chemistry laboratory operations into three interconnected levels: Atomic Skills, which characterize fundamental dexterous manipulation capabilities; Compositional Skills; and Long-Horizon Laboratory Workflows. This hierarchical design not only supports the evaluation of end-task performance, but also enables the analysis of how fundamental dexterous skills compose and influence more complex laboratory operations. We conduct cross-level evaluations of representative robot learning methods in both real-world and simulation environments. The experimental results validate the effectiveness of the LabDex task design and demonstration data, and show that the benchmark supports the training and systematic evaluation of existing robotic policies across laboratory dexterous manipulation tasks at different levels, providing a foundation for further research and development of autonomous laboratory robots.

</details>


### [119] [Evaluation of Monocular SLAM Systems on High-Altitude Nadir UAV Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18632)
*Gašper Spagnolo,Matej Dobrevski,Danijel Skočaj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地评估五种单目SLAM系统在航空正射视频（aerial nadir video）中的性能表现，涵盖无人机局部飞行、合成城市尺度图像和长距离航空序列。结果表明MASt3R-SLAM在DJI短航迹上表现最佳（平均水平MAE为参考路径长度的0.53%），DROID-SLAM在整体完成轨迹中性能最好（平均2.88%），但所有系统在长距离航迹上均无法保持全局形状一致，垂直定位精度差，单目SLAM目前不足以独立支持可靠的纯视觉航空导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空正射视频场景具有弱几何约束和严重感知混淆（perceptual aliasing）的特点，是单目SLAM的困难场景。此前缺乏针对该场景系统的多系统基准评估，作者希望通过benchmark揭示当前单目SLAM在纯视觉航空导航中的瓶颈。

Method: 对五种单目SLAM系统（包括MASt3R-SLAM和DROID-SLAM等）进行基准测试，使用三类数据集：五次本地无人机（DJI）飞行、合成城市尺度（city-scale）图像、以及长距离航空序列（GES和ALTO）。实验设计上刻意去除了惯性（IMU）和GNSS辅助，以纯粹隔离视觉SLAM的性能。评估指标包括平均水平MAE（与参考路径长度的百分比）以及全局轨迹形状保持能力。

Result: 性能随环境和轨迹尺度变化显著：MASt3R-SLAM在五次DJI飞行中取得最低平均水平MAE（0.53%参考路径长度）；在长GES和ALTO序列上没有任何系统能一致保持全局轨迹形状；综合已完成运行的轨迹，DROID-SLAM表现最佳，平均误差为参考路径长度的2.88%。垂直位置精度普遍较差，大范围轨迹即使具备闭环能力仍然高度失真。

Conclusion: 当前单目SLAM方法单独使用时不足以提供可靠的纯视觉航空导航能力。尽管在短程场景中某些系统表现良好，但在长距离航空序列中的全局轨迹失真和垂直定位误差仍然构成根本性挑战，需要进一步的研究或引入额外信息源。

Abstract: Aerial nadir video combines weak geometric constraints with severe perceptual aliasing, making it a difficult regime for monocular SLAM. We benchmark five monocular SLAM systems on local UAV flights, synthetic city-scale imagery, and long-range aerial sequences. To isolate visual performance, we provide no inertial or GNSS aiding. Performance varies strongly with environment and trajectory scale: MASt3R-SLAM achieves the lowest mean horizontal MAE on the five DJI flights (0.53\% of reference path length), whereas no system consistently preserves global trajectory shape on the long GES and ALTO sequences. Overall, DROID-SLAM performs best, averaging 2.88\% of reference path length across completed runs. Vertical position remains poor, and large-area trajectories remain highly distorted despite loop-closure capability. Current monocular SLAM methods are by themselves therefore insufficient for reliable visual-only aerial navigation.

</details>


### [120] [Progressive Experience Fusion for Multi-Task World Model Control in Endovascular Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18647)
*Harry Robertshaw,Maxence Boels,Nikola Fischer,Sebastien Ourselin,Christos Bergeles,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出渐进式经验融合（PEF）方法训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器，用于自主血管内导航以支持机械取栓术。PEF在五个子任务中平均成功率达74%，优于SAC（37%）和基础TD-MPC2（65%）；结合自适应规划视界后在30个血管解剖结构上训练，于10个未见血管中达到90%成功率；并成功迁移至体外卒中患者血管模型，微调后路径比从63%提升至80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主血管内导航有望将机械取栓术推广至医疗资源匮乏地区，但控制器需要穿越多变血管解剖结构中的长距离多阶段路径，现有方法在跨血管解剖泛化和患者特异适应方面能力不足。

Method: 提出了渐进式经验融合（PEF）方法训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器；评估了一种基于残差动作序列离散度动态调整Model Predictive Path Integral规划视界的启发式策略；并在患者特异仿真中进行微调以提升迁移表现。

Result: PEF在五个子任务中平均成功率74%（SAC为37%，p<0.001；基础TD-MPC2为65%，p=0.053）；结合自适应视界规划在30个血管上训练后，10个未见血管中成功率达90%；在体外卒中患者血管模型上，微调后路径比从63%升至80%（p<0.001），仅需4万步微调（约107分钟临床转运时间）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了多血管解剖训练与患者特异适应相结合的概念验证，展示了自主血管内导航在临床场景中应用的潜力，但需进一步验证后方可进入临床部署。

Abstract: Autonomous endovascular navigation could support the delivery of mechanical thrombectomy to underserved areas, but controllers must navigate long, multi-stage paths across varying vascular anatomies. This study investigates Progressive Experience Fusion (PEF) to train a multi-task TD-MPC2 controller. We additionally evaluate a heuristic that changes the Model Predictive Path Integral planning horizon using residual action-sequence dispersion, and fine-tuning in a patient-specific simulation. Across five subtasks in ten known training anatomies with held-out targets, PEF achieved a mean success rate of 74%, compared with 37% for Soft Actor-Critic (p < 0.001) and 65% for base TD-MPC2 (p = 0.053). A PEF controller with adaptive-horizon planning trained on 30 vasculatures achieved a mean success rate of 90% in ten held-out vasculatures. The PEF agent successfully transferred to an unseen in vitro stroke patient vasculature under fluoroscopy, achieving a mean path ratio improvement from 63% to 80% with fine-tuning (p < 0.001), following 40x103 fine-tuning steps (corresponding to approximately 107 min of clinical inter-hospital transfer time). This work represents a proof of concept for multi-vasculature training and patient-specific adaptation, while further validation is required before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [121] [Orienteering Problem with Uncertain Time-Varying Rewards: Framework and Benchmark for Everyday Service Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18672)
*Masafumi Endo,Kohei Honda,Yuu Jinnai,Ryo Yonetani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出带有不确定时变奖励的定向越野问题（OP-UTVR），允许智能体从观测中估计奖励动态并预测未来奖励，通过三种不同规划策略（规划时域与在线自适应不同）来解决，并在移动服务机器人基准上验证了长时域规划与在线自适应结合的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定向越野问题（OP）大多假设奖励事先已知，但实际应用中奖励往往是不确定且随时间变化的（例如送货员的客户需求变化），因此需要一种能够处理不确定时变奖励的OP变体。

Method: 提出OP-UTVR问题，采用三种在规划时域和在线自适应性上不同的规划器来解决该问题，并推导了在奖励随机性下这些规划器性能的理论界。同时引入一个移动服务机器人在室内环境中于行人之间导航的基准测试。

Result: 实验揭示了规划时域与自适应性之间的权衡，并展示了长时域规划结合在线适应策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 长时域规划与在线适应的组合在OP-UTVR中表现最佳，为处理不确定时变奖励的路径规划问题提供了理论与实践指导。

Abstract: We present the orienteering problem with uncertain time-varying rewards (OP-UTVR), a novel variant of the orienteering problem (OP). While most existing OP formulations assume rewards to be known in advance, practical applications involve uncertain and time-varying rewards, as with shifting customer demand for delivery agents. OP-UTVR relaxes this assumption by allowing agents to estimate reward dynamics from observations and forecast future rewards. This enables informed routing decisions despite stochastic reward changes and inevitable prediction errors. We address this problem using three planners that differ in planning horizon and online adaptivity, and derive theoretical bounds on their performance under reward stochasticity. We further introduce a mobile service robot benchmark for OP-UTVR, where a robot navigates among pedestrians in indoor environments. Experiments reveal trade-offs between planning horizon and adaptivity, and demonstrate the effectiveness of long-horizon planning with online adaptation.

</details>


### [122] [SoftVTBench: A Deformation-Aware Visuo-Tactile Dataset and Benchmark for Deformable-Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18701)
*Bowen Jing,Mingxin Wang,Ruiyang Hao,Chenchen Ge,Hanwen Shen,Junjie He,Yang Cui,Yiming Hou,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Dandan Zhang,Ding Zhao,Houde Liu,Xiaofan Li,Si Liu,Ping Luo,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftVTBench是一个面向物理交互感知的软体物体操控视觉-触觉数据集与闭环基准。包含4,000条专家演示和50多个资产，以20Hz同步多视角RGB、双指触觉RGB与标记运动、本体感觉、语言指令及动作，并配有限元仿真状态作为评估金标准。基准引入形变感知成功率(DSR)，要求任务完成且峰值归一化形变在容差内。实验表明，现有策略存在大量"成功但违规"的rollout，且仅提供触觉信号并不能保证有效的多模态融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体物体操控基准大多只评估任务成功与否，忽略了物理交互质量（如滑移、过度压缩等）。缺乏将策略可见的接触观测与独立物理真值配对的全任务视觉-触觉数据集，成为研究瓶颈。

Method: 构建SoftVTBench数据集（4,000条演示、50+资产、20Hz多模态同步+有限元状态），并提出基于固定物体特定标定的形变感知成功率(DSR)基准，对Diffusion Policy、π0.5和FastWAM三种策略在12种分布内配置及分布偏移下进行系统评估，比较有无触觉输入的性能差异。

Result: 所有12个分布内配置均存在违反形变容差的成功rollout，占各配置成功率的0.7-24%。分布偏移下，视觉-触觉变体在全部6个策略-套件对比中任务成功率更高、其中5个DSR更高，但分布内增益有限，说明触觉并非自动带来多模态融合优势。

Conclusion: SoftVTBench作为公共视觉-触觉资源，支持研究策略不仅是否成功，还研究其如何物理交互软体物体以及触觉何时能真正改善这种交互。

Abstract: Physical interaction quality is central to deformable-object manipulation, yet most benchmarks evaluate task success alone. A policy may complete the task while allowing slip or causing excessive compression. A primary bottleneck is the absence of visuo-tactile datasets that pair policy-visible contact observations with independent physical ground truth over complete tasks. We introduce SoftVTBench, a visuo-tactile dataset for physical-interaction-aware deformable-object manipulation. It contains 4,000 expert demonstrations and more than 50 assets, including volumetric deformable objects and visually matched rigid twins. At 20 Hz, each episode synchronizes multi-view RGB, dual-finger tactile RGB and marker motion, proprioception, language, and binary and continuous gripper actions, alongside evaluator-only finite-element (FEM) states. Building upon this dataset, we establish a closed-loop benchmark that uses fixed object-specific calibration to define the Deformation-aware Success Rate (DSR), which counts a rollout as successful only when it completes the task and keeps peak normalized deformation within tolerance. Across Diffusion Policy, $π_{0.5}$, and FastWAM, all 12 in-distribution configurations contain successful rollouts that violate the deformation tolerance, accounting for 0.7--24% of each configuration's successes. Under distribution shift, visuo-tactile variants achieve higher task success in all six policy--suite comparisons and higher DSR in five, whereas their in-distribution benefits are mixed. These results show that making touch available does not by itself ensure effective multimodal fusion. SoftVTBench therefore provides a common visuo-tactile resource for studying not only whether a policy succeeds, but how it physically interacts with deformable objects and when touch improves that interaction.

</details>


### [123] [Dream2Reward: Transition-Alignment Reward Models from Positive Demonstrations for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18787)
*Haoyu Zhang,Zecui Zeng,Bin Wang,Lusong Li,Liang Lin,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dream2Reward提出了一种从正样本演示中学习语言条件化的成功隐状态迁移场的方法，通过对比实际动作与预测的成功变化来生成稠密因果奖励，无需失败标注或进度标签，在机制诊断和真实机器人操作中均优于基于进度的奖励方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于进度的奖励在行为偏离成功示范时可能给出高奖励（例如错误转换后仍显示进展），导致奖励黑客问题。需要一种不依赖失败标注、能在观测看似进步但实际错误时仍提供信息性反馈的稠密奖励机制。

Method: Dream2Reward学习一个语言条件化的成功隐状态迁移场：给定转换开始前的视觉历史，模型预测成功执行对应的隐状态位移，并通过有符号方向一致性和对称幅度一致性来评分观测到的位移。这种迁移级比较可惩罚错误方向、过冲和停滞运动。

Result: 在机制诊断和共享轨迹评估中，Dream2Reward比基于进度的替代方法提供了更强的成功-失败区分度和更信息性的低质量行为反馈。在线和离线策略学习中，同一冻结奖励模型减少了奖励黑客并支持更强的下游性能，包括真实机器人操作。

Conclusion: 将实际运动与预测的成功变化进行比较，是将正样本演示转化为机器人学习稠密奖励的有效方式，无需任何额外标注即可实现稳健的奖励信号。

Abstract: Learning robotic policies requires dense rewards that remain informative when behavior departs from successful demonstrations. Progress-based rewards estimate how far an observation has advanced along a nominal successful trajectory, but may remain high after an incorrect transition. We introduce Dream2Reward, which learns a language-conditioned successful latent transition field from positive demonstrations. Given the visual history up to a transition start, the model predicts the latent displacement associated with successful execution and scores the observed displacement through signed directional and symmetric magnitude agreement. This transition-level comparison penalizes wrong-direction, overshooting, and stagnant motion even when the resulting observation appears to show progress. Dream2Reward requires no failure annotations, progress labels, or synthetic negatives, and produces a dense causal reward. Across mechanism diagnostics and shared-trajectory evaluations, it provides stronger success-failure separation and more informative feedback on low-quality behavior than progress-based alternatives. Across online and offline policy learning, the same frozen reward model reduces reward hacking and supports stronger downstream performance, including in real-robot manipulation. These results show that comparing realized motion with predicted successful change provides an effective way to convert positive demonstrations into dense rewards for robot learning.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond Placement and Articulation: Usage-Driven Code Scenes for Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18840)
*Zijian Xiao,Zipeng Ye,Jinkun Hao,Xiong Yang,Yuchen Xie,Ran Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoomWright是一个基于代理的、以使用为导向的3D场景生成框架，将场景完全表示为代码，用于具身交互。它通过使用驱动的对象推理、触发-条件-效果的交互规则编译以及注释引导的定向机制，生成可执行、可编辑且可直接用于仿真的交互环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代码的场景生成方法仅关注视觉构建和对象级关节，忽略了场景的功能性使用，即场景中对象如何被交互和操作未被建模，限制了其在具身AI和策略学习中的应用。

Method: 1) 使用驱动的对象推理：将每个锚点视为任务中心，引入任务所需对象及其可供性（affordance）；2) 代码代理实现多部件交互：将每次交互编译为触发-条件-效果规则，更新结构化对象状态，捕获对象间的因果依赖；3) 注释引导的使用导向定向：解决可操作对象方向模糊难以从像素恢复的问题。

Result: 大量实验证明了方法的有效性，生成的场景可执行、可编辑、仿真就绪，为具身AI和策略学习提供了交互环境。

Conclusion: RoomWright填补了代码化场景生成中功能性交互建模的空白，将场景从静态结构提升为可交互的动态环境，为具身AI和策略学习提供更实用的仿真平台。

Abstract: Indoor scene synthesis provides essential environments for embodied AI, robotic manipulation, and simulation-based policy learning. Recent code-based scene generation methods produce editable and extensible environments, yet they remain focused on visual construction and object-level articulation, leaving the functional usage of scenes largely unmodeled. To address this problem, we present RoomWright, an agentic usage-driven framework for generating 3D scenes represented entirely as code for embodied interaction. RoomWright performs usage-driven object reasoning, which treats each anchor as a task centre and admits task-required objects and their affordances. A code agent further enables multi-part interaction by compiling each interaction into a trigger, condition, effect rule that updates structured object states, capturing causal dependencies across objects. Moreover, since manipuland orientation is ambiguous and hard to recover from pixels, RoomWright alleviates this via annotation-informed usage-guided orientation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. The resulting scenes are executable, editable, and simulation-ready, providing interactive environments for embodied AI and policy learning.

</details>


### [125] [RoboEdit: Turning Human Manipulation Videos into Scalable Robot Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18948)
*Yaowei Guo,Zeng Tao,Yuxin Jiang,Yunuo Chen,Zhiyang Dou,Yuxiang Ma,Yin Yang,Demetri Terzopoulos,Ying Jiang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboEdit，一个将人类操作视频转化为动作一致、物理可行的机器人视频的编辑套件，包含自动数据生成管线RoboEdit-ADC、大规模数据集RoboEdit-14M和核心编辑引擎RoboEdit-Trans，实现跨具身视频编辑并支持下游机器人控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收集机器人手-物交互数据成本高昂且具有具身特异性，而海量人类操作视频无法直接用于机器人训练，缺乏可扩展的跨具身数据利用方案。

Method: RoboEdit包含三个核心组件：(1) RoboEdit-ADC自动管线，从RGB视频中重建并重定向3D交互到不同具身；(2) RoboEdit-14M大规模数据集，包含174K对齐视频对（14M帧），覆盖7种机器人具身和多种场景；(3) RoboEdit-Trans编辑引擎，采用跨具身适应模块保持时间一致性，并集成3D Robot-State Decoder恢复逐帧手部状态用于结构化运动监督。

Result: 实验表明RoboEdit在编辑质量上达到最先进水平，并支持真实世界操作任务中的下游机器人控制策略。

Conclusion: RoboEdit套件解锁了无标注人类视频的巨大潜力，为通用机器人学习提供可扩展、高保真的视觉和3D运动监督。

Abstract: Collecting robot hand-object interaction data is costly and embodiment-specific, yet abundant human-object videos remain unusable for robot training. We present RoboEdit, a human-to-robot video editing suite that transforms human manipulation videos into action-consistent, physically plausible robot videos with aligned 3D hand states. To enable scalable supervision, we introduce RoboEdit-ADC, an automatic pipeline that reconstructs and retargets 3D interactions from RGB videos across embodiments. This pipeline generates RoboEdit-14M, a large-scale dataset of 174K aligned video pairs (14M frames) spanning seven robot embodiments, diverse scenes, and interaction types. The core editing engine, RoboEdit-Trans, employs cross-embodiment adaptation modules to preserve temporal coherence while adapting appearance and motion. It further integrates a 3D Robot-State Decoder to recover per-frame hand states for structured motion supervision. Experiments show that RoboEdit achieves state-of-the-art editing quality and supports downstream robot control policies in real-world manipulation tasks. Ultimately, the RoboEdit suite unlocks the vast potential of unlabeled human videos, providing scalable, high-fidelity visual and 3D motion supervision for generalizable robot learning.

</details>


### [126] [Autonomous Agricultural Tractor: Integrated Weed Detection and LiDAR Navigation for Precision Paddy Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19004)
*Benjamin Merryman-Smith,Tony Nguyen,Bilal Dogutas,Krish Shah,Anthony Raphael,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgriNav，一个集成式自主拖拉机系统，通过四个ROS耦合模块（基于PyTorch重实现的WeedDet水稻检测、1.68M参数的CNN-FPN变体、非对称逻辑判别模块和六级状态EKF）解决水稻田定点杂草管理中的三大挑战：冠层下GNSS退化时的作物行导航、水稻与杂草的实时视觉区分、以及将水稻误判为杂草的不可逆成本问题。系统核心贡献是四机制LiDAR-相机融合桥，利用导航LiDAR实现ROI约束、世界坐标投影、地面平面滤波和双向置信度融合，零额外硬件成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水稻田定点杂草管理可大幅减少除草剂使用，但受限于三个持续挑战：GNSS在冠层下退化导致的导航难题、水稻与形态多样的杂草之间的实时视觉区分困难、以及将水稻误判为杂草的不可逆不对称成本。这些挑战阻碍了该技术的田间部署。

Method: AgriNav由四个ROS耦合模块构成：(1)自定义PyTorch重实现的WeedDet用于水稻检测；(2)带非对称类别权重的轻量1.68M参数CNN-FPN变体；(3)通过硬编码置信度门控否决机制保护水稻类别的反逻辑判别模块；(4)融合GNSS、IMU和轮式里程计的三级中断桥接六状态恒速转弯率扩展卡尔曼滤波器。系统级核心贡献是四机制LiDAR-相机融合桥：利用导航LiDAR进行ROI约束、世界坐标投影、地面平面滤波及双向置信度融合。

Result: 仿真实验表明：系统可在20秒GNSS中断期间保持连续位置跟踪，作物行检测置信度全程超过0.9，水稻检测置信度在水稻、航拍和洪水后图像中达到0.32至0.95。LiDAR ROI约束将检测推理区域缩减约30%至50%。

Conclusion: AgriNav通过创新的LiDAR-相机融合机制和不对称保护策略，在不增加硬件成本的前提下有效解决了水稻田定点杂草管理的三大挑战，为实际田间部署提供了可行的自主导航与精准识别方案。

Abstract: Site-specific weed management in paddy farming offers substantial reductions in herbicide use over conventional broadcast spraying, but field deployment has been limited by three persistent challenges: robust crop-row navigation under canopy where GNSS degrades, real-time visual discrimination between rice and morphologically diverse weeds, and the asymmetric cost of misclassifying rice as weed, which is irreversible.
  This paper presents AgriNav, an integrated autonomous tractor system built around four ROS-coupled modules: a custom PyTorch reimplementation of WeedDet for rice detection, a parallel lightweight 1.68M-parameter CNN-FPN variant with asymmetric class weighting, an inverted-logic discrimination module that protects the rice class through a hardcoded confidence-gate veto, and a 6-state constant-velocity-turn-rate Extended Kalman Filter fusing GNSS, IMU, and wheel odometry with three-level outage bridging. Our primary system-level contribution is a four-mechanism LiDAR-camera fusion bridge that uses the navigation LiDAR for region-of-interest constraint, world-coordinate projection, ground-plane filtering, and bidirectional confidence fusion at zero additional hardware cost.
  Simulation experiments demonstrate continuous position tracking through a 20-second GNSS outage, crop row detection confidence above 0.9 throughout operation, and rice-detection confidences from 0.32 to 0.95 across paddy, aerial, and post-flood imagery. The LiDAR ROI constraint reduces detection inference region by an estimated 30 to 50 percent.

</details>


### [127] [LT-Mem: Volatility-Aware Spatio-Temporal Memory for Lifelong Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19059)
*Yumin Lee,Hyoseok Ju,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LT-Mem，一个波动感知的记忆演进框架，通过统一空间对齐的实例级3D感知与波动条件的时间推理，解决长期机器人操作中跨会话对象历史丢失（时间遗忘）的问题。系统包含多会话SLAM骨干、波动感知策略驱动的记忆演进层，以及Live/Delta/Meta三记忆结构，并引入LT-VQA数据集进行评测，在全部指标上超越基线且消耗更少的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期的机器人操作系统中，要么覆盖历史以维护最新地图（丢失历史），要么存储语义快照但缺乏跨会话的对象身份一致性，导致所谓的"时间遗忘"——系统性地丢失对象历史信息，无法回答诸如"绿色椅子在所有会话中出现在哪里"这类跨时间查询问题。

Method: LT-Mem包含三个核心组件：(1) 多会话SLAM骨干网络，提供跨会话空间对齐的实例级3D观测；(2) 记忆演进推理层，采用确定性证据评分保持跨会话身份，并通过波动感知策略在覆盖（overwrite）、保持（hold）和多假设（multi-hypothesis）三种动作间选择；(3) 三记忆结构（Live、Delta、Meta），同时保存当前状态和事件历史，支持纵向对象中心推理。此外构建LT-VQA数据集，包含多会话记录、持久身份标注和时间问答对。

Result: 实验表明LT-Mem在所有指标上持续优于基线方法，同时消耗比基线少一个数量级的token。消融实验证实性能提升主要来自结构化记忆架构本身，而非LLM能力的增强。

Conclusion: LT-Mem通过设计波动感知的结构化记忆架构，有效解决了长期机器人操作中的对象历史记忆问题，实现了高效且准确的时间性对象中心推理，为长期自主机器人系统提供了可扩展的记忆管理范式。

Abstract: Long-term robot operation in evolving environments requires object-level understanding that persists across repeated revisits. Existing systems either overwrite history to maintain an up-to-date map or store semantic snapshots without consistent cross-session object identity, resulting in temporal amnesia: the systematic loss of object history that prevents answering queries such as "Where has the green chair been across all sessions?" We propose LT-Mem, a volatility-aware memory evolution framework that unifies spatially aligned instance-level 3D perception with volatility-conditioned temporal reasoning. First, a multi-session SLAM backbone provides spatially aligned per-object observations across sessions. Second, a reasoning layer governs how object memory evolves: deterministic evidence scoring preserves cross-session identity, and a volatility-aware policy selects among overwrite, hold, and multi-hypothesis actions based on each object's dynamics. Third, the resulting Tri-Memory structure (Live, Delta, Meta) preserves both current states and event histories, enabling longitudinal object-centric reasoning. We further introduce LT-VQA, a dataset and evaluation suite comprising multi-session recordings, persistent identity annotations, and temporal QA pairs. Experiments show that LT-Mem consistently outperforms baselines across all metrics while consuming an order of magnitude fewer tokens, and ablations confirm that gains are driven by the structured memory architecture rather than LLM capacity.

</details>


### [128] [DA-WAM: Decision-Aligned Future Latents for Driving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19085)
*Ruiguo Zhong,Benshan Ma,Xiaolong Chen,Lang Zhang,Mingyue Feng,Yaonong Wang,Pei Liu,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DA-WAM框架，将预测性表示学习、动作条件未来建模和轨迹评分统一到单一决策目标下，在NAVSIM-v1和NAVSIM-v2上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型在决策支持方面潜力未充分挖掘。关键问题在于未来建模应当是"决策信息性"的——预测的未来必须直接指导轨迹选择。现有方法要么将未来表示学习与规划优化解耦，要么在不同轨迹候选间共享预测状态，从而稀释了应指导选择的行为特定后果。

Method: DA-WAM框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过在线编码器和稳定的动量目标在整个规划优化过程中维持预测监督，使未来表示与驾驶任务共同演化；(2) 动作条件预测器为每个轨迹候选生成不同的未来潜在状态；(3) 未来潜在状态条件化的因子化评分器评估各候选轨迹。专家匹配轨迹的未来潜在状态由观测的未来表示监督，安全关键的硬负样本在规划边界附近提供额外监督。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v1和NAVSIM-v2上均达到最先进性能，消融实验和诊断分析验证了各关键组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将预测性表示学习、动作条件未来建模与轨迹评分统一到单一决策目标中，DA-WAM弥合了未来建模与规划优化之间的鸿沟，使预测的未来能够直接且有辨别性地指导自动驾驶系统中的轨迹选择。

Abstract: Anticipating how scenes evolve under ego actions is fundamental to safe autonomous driving, yet the full potential of world models for decision-making remains unrealized. The critical challenge lies in ensuring that future modeling is not merely predictive, but decision-informative: the predicted future must directly shape which trajectory is selected. Existing approaches decouple future representation learning from planning optimization, or share predicted states across trajectory candidates, thereby diluting the action-specific consequences that ought to guide selection. To bridge this gap, we propose DA-WAM, a framework that unifies predictive representation learning, action-conditioned future modeling, and trajectory scoring under a single decision-making objective. DA-WAM maintains predictive supervision throughout planner optimization via an online encoder and a stable momentum target, allowing future representations to co-evolve with the driving task. An action-conditioned predictor generates a distinct future latent state per trajectory candidate, which is then evaluated by a future-latent-conditioned factorized scorer. For the expert-matched trajectory, the predicted future latent is supervised by the observed future representation, while safety-critical hard negatives provide additional supervision near planning boundaries. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM-v1 and NAVSIM-v2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, while ablations and diagnostic analyses validate the key components.

</details>


### [129] [ADEPT: Accelerating Dexterity via Pre-Training and Post-Training using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19182)
*Jayjun Lee,Jessica Yin,Asif Rana,Nicholas Blauch,Sam Mady,Mohak Bhardwaj,Nima Fazeli,Nathan Ratliff,Karl Van Wyk,Ankur Handa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ADEPT是一个大规模强化学习框架，通过在通用物体重定位任务上预训练灵巧策略，再将其作为先验进行下游任务的后训练，实现了高自由度多指机器人上从原始视觉-触觉感知直接学习长时程任务的sim-to-real迁移，并在两个不同机器人平台上实现了人类速度水平的灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自由度多指机器人的灵巧操作任务通常难以从零开始通过强化学习发现，且每个下游任务都需要重新学习相同的技能集合。现有方法缺乏一种可跨任务复用的预训练机制，同时朴素RL微调会在迁移时迅速破坏预训练获得的能力。

Method: ADEPT首先在通用物体重定位任务上预训练灵巧策略，然后采用稳定的后训练流程，包括行为克隆蒸馏、评论家预热和保守的on-policy更新，以避免微调过程中预训练能力的退化。此外引入关节空间Geometric Fabric作为RL策略与机器人之间的中介，以安全利用完整运动学灵巧性；最后将后训练得到的教师策略蒸馏为感知学生策略，实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在23自由度的Kuka-Allegro（双RGB相机）和29自由度的Flexiv-Sharpa（双RGB相机加五个视觉触觉传感器）两个平台上实现了零样本sim-to-real迁移，能够从具有挑战性的初始状态解决长时程任务，且操作速度达到人类水平。

Conclusion: ADEPT证明了通过通用预训练+稳定后训练+教师-学生蒸馏的框架，可以有效实现跨实施例、跨任务的灵巧操作技能复用和真实世界迁移，避免了对每个新任务从头学习同一组技能的浪费。

Abstract: We introduce Accelerating Dexterity via Pre-Training (ADEPT), a large-scale reinforcement learning (RL) framework for learning sim-to-real transferable dexterity across high degree-of-freedom (DoF) robot embodiments that can solve long-horizon tasks directly from raw visuo-tactile perception. ADEPT pretrains a dexterous policy on a generic object reposing task, then post-trains downstream policies with this pretrained behavior as a prior. ADEPT enables learning new behaviors that are otherwise difficult to discover from scratch on multi-fingered robots and avoids learning the same set of skills over again for every new downstream task. The pretrained policy zero-shots the reposing phase of downstream tasks, but naïve RL fine-tuning rapidly degrades this capability during transfer. We address this with a stable post-training recipe combining behavior-cloning distillation, critic warm-up, and conservative on-policy updates. To safely exploit the full kinematic dexterity, we introduce a joint-space Geometric Fabric that mediates between the RL policy and the robot. We distill post-trained teachers into perceptive students that zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on two embodiments: a 23 DoF Kuka-Allegro with two RGB cameras, and a 29 DoF Flexiv-Sharpa with two RGB cameras and five vision-based tactile sensors, and can solve long-horizon tasks from challenging initial states with dexterity at human-level speed.

</details>


### [130] [PartialBiGrasp: Inferring Hidden Local Geometry for Bimanual Grasping from Partial Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19188)
*Ayush Kaura,Vignesh Vembar,Md Faizal Karim,Keshab Patra,K Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PartialBiGrasp，一种直接基于部分点云观测的双臂抓取生成框架，通过卷积占位网络隐式学习几何特征，生成力闭合合规的双臂抓取对，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证了其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型、重型、几何复杂的物体需要双臂抓取，但这类物体往往只有稀疏的可抓取区域。现有双手抓取方法假设能获取物体的完整点云，这在真实场景中通常不可行，因此需要一种能直接从部分点云观测中生成双臂抓取方案的方法。

Method: PartialBiGrasp利用卷积占位网络（convolutional occupancy networks）隐式学习几何特征，实现对可抓取性、无碰撞接触区域和物体厚度的局部推理；基于这些理解生成力闭合（force-closure）合规的抓取对，并通过基于采样的优化方法进一步精修，以修正因几何不完整带来的歧义。

Result: 通过解析力闭合指标、大规模仿真实验以及真实机器人对含噪声的部分点云新物体的评估，证明了该方法能够生成鲁棒且物理稳定的双臂抓取。

Conclusion: PartialBiGrasp在仅依赖部分点云观测的情况下，依然能够实现稳健可靠的双臂抓取生成，为真实场景中大型物体的双臂操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dual-arm robotic grasping is essential for manipulating large, heavy, and geometrically complex objects that cannot be reliably handled using a single manipulator. These large objects often contain only sparse graspable regions determined by local geometric properties such as thickness, edge structure, and gripper clearance. Prior bimanual grasping methods assume access to a full point cloud of the object which inherently contains this geometric information, but may not be accessible in real scenarios. This work proposes PartialBiGrasp, a dual-arm grasp generation framework that operates directly on partial point cloud observations. Our model learns geometric features implicitly through convolutional occupancy networks, enabling local reasoning about graspability, collision-free contact regions, and object thickness. We leverage this understanding to generate force-closure compliant grasp pairs, which are further refined using a sampling-based optimization to correct for ambiguity caused by incomplete geometry. We evaluate our approach using analytical force-closure metrics, large-scale simulation experiments, and real-world robot evaluations on noisy partial point clouds of novel objects, demonstrating robust and physically stable dual-arm grasp generation.

</details>
